64:T139ce,

import { BlockInfoCard } from "@/components/ui/block-info-card"

<BlockInfoCard type="rootly" color="#6C72C8" />

Nutzungsanweisungen [#nutzungsanweisungen]

Integrate Rootly incident management into workflows. Create and manage incidents, alerts, services, severities, and retrospectives.

Tools [#tools]

rootly_create_incident [#rootly_create_incident]

Create a new incident in Rootly with optional severity, services, and teams.

Eingabe [#eingabe]

| Parameter          | Typ     | Erforderlich | Beschreibung                                                                                                                              |
| ------------------ | ------- | ------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `apiKey`           | string  | Ja           | Rootly API key                                                                                                                            |
| `title`            | string  | Nein         | The title of the incident (auto-generated if not provided)                                                                                |
| `summary`          | string  | Nein         | A summary of the incident                                                                                                                 |
| `severityId`       | string  | Nein         | Severity ID to attach to the incident                                                                                                     |
| `status`           | string  | Nein         | Incident status (in\_triage, started, detected, acknowledged, mitigated, resolved, closed, cancelled, scheduled, in\_progress, completed) |
| `kind`             | string  | Nein         | Incident kind (normal, normal\_sub, test, test\_sub, example, example\_sub, backfilled, scheduled, scheduled\_sub)                        |
| `serviceIds`       | string  | Nein         | Comma-separated service IDs to attach                                                                                                     |
| `environmentIds`   | string  | Nein         | Comma-separated environment IDs to attach                                                                                                 |
| `groupIds`         | string  | Nein         | Comma-separated team/group IDs to attach                                                                                                  |
| `incidentTypeIds`  | string  | Nein         | Comma-separated incident type IDs to attach                                                                                               |
| `functionalityIds` | string  | Nein         | Comma-separated functionality IDs to attach                                                                                               |
| `labels`           | string  | Nein         | Labels as JSON object, e.g. \{"platform":"osx","version":"1.29"}                                                                          |
| `private`          | boolean | Nein         | Create as a private incident (cannot be undone)                                                                                           |
